## Change ledger

- **2026-10-27 — opus-vl-fit-planner — Voice Lab fit planner (§8.9.2):** additive Core contract. `src/VoxClip.Core/Domain/VoiceLab/VoiceLabFitPlanner.cs` adds public `VoiceLabFitTake`, `VoiceLabFitEditKind`, `VoiceLabFitEdit`, `VoiceLabFitSeam`, `VoiceLabFitPlan`, `VoiceLabFitResult`, `VoiceLabFitResult.Exact`, `VoiceLabFitResult.ChangedDuration` and `VoiceLabFitPlanner`. `VoiceLabPatchConstants` gains seven §8.3 constants, and `VoiceLabGeometryErrorCode` gains `FitTouchesVoice` at its end. Review round 2 adds the public `VoiceLabWindowPlanner.LendableSilence(Project, VoiceLabOutputScope, VoiceLabWindow)`. Nothing is serialized, and no consumer changes. `VoiceLabTempoException` keeps its type and every catch; only its default message changed (§10.5).

- **2026-10-27 — opus-vl-fit-planner — Deferred: rename `VoiceLabTempoException`:** the lead deferred this rename in review round 2 (decision 9): matching the Mac refusal's message is enough for now. A later rename would make the public `VoxClip.Media.VoiceLab.VoiceLabTempoException` `VoiceLabNonunityFitException`, or another name the lead approves, so the type names the permanent §10.5 refusal.
  - Owner: the Media Voice Lab track.
  - Affected consumers: `src/VoxClip.App/ViewModels/UiError.cs:28` (App) and `CliApplication.RunAsync` in `src/VoxClip.Cli/CliApplication.cs` (CLI), plus the Media sources and the three Media test files that throw or assert it.
  - No JSON, fixture or wire value changes.
  - Reason: lead decision 6 of round 1 asked for the rename. Rule 3 requires producer and consumers to land together, and the App file is outside this track, so round 1 changed only the message, and round 2 deferred the rename.
- **2026-10-27 — opus-win-redesign — Round 1e lead decisions:**
  - **Swift Core parity (lead-approved Swift Core change):** VoxClipCore gains `EditingAlgorithms` (`clipTitleWords`, `clipTitle(_ words: some Sequence<String>) -> String?`) in `apps/macos/Sources/VoxClipCore/Domain/Editing/EditingAlgorithms.swift`, the same rule as `EditingAlgorithms.ClipTitle` here, and a swift-testing `ManualClipTitleTests` that replays `shared/fixtures/clips/manual-titles.json`. The Mac App is unchanged; its consumer action is in the round-1d entry below and in apps/macos/CONTRACTS.md.
  - **2026-10-27 — sol-win-redesign — Relink replaces the temporary deviation:** Home and the media-missing editor notice offer Locate…. Windows Core now exposes `IProjectStore.RelinkAsync`, `ProjectRelinkResult`, `Relinking.Decide` and `RelinkDecision`. Mac Core extracts the same pure decision without changing its store behavior. Both replay `shared/fixtures/projects/relink-cases.json`. Matching identity is silent; a different file needs “This file looks different. Use it anyway?” and the existing 0.05-second planning-duration bound. The atomic writer is unchanged and is called once per accepted relink; its next save recalculates relativePath. Unresolved projects block document mutation; accepted relinks leave read-only state. Export and Ctrl+E restore “Locate the video to export.”.
  - **Home acts on no hidden editor:** while Home shows, the project's clip, suggestion, Suggested and none-found rows only navigate. `SidebarRow.Edits` is false there; the window disables Export clip…, Rename…, Remove clip, Keep clip, Dismiss, the Keep and Dismiss buttons, Keep all and Find clips…, and ignores Delete, Ctrl+Enter and F2. Copy text stays.
  - `ShortcutPolicy.RowClipStep` accepts the Full take row, so ] from Full take selects clip 1 (design §6.2). `EditorViewModel.ExportBlocked` judges the requested clip's own scope, not the active one, and a blocked Ctrl+E or Export clip… only announces its reason, so the notice showing stays.
  - Find clips and its callout's Retry re-read `llm.connector` before the preflight (AI spec §11 gate 4); an empty or unknown setting shows §13 "Clips: no connector" (`ClipCallouts.Chosen`). Only the native walk exercises the re-read, so Retry after a connector change is an open native-walk check; `ChosenConnectorComesFromTheCurrentSetting` covers the choice itself.
  - **Pre-merge lead fixes (verification review r1e):**
    - With no transcript, the Full take exports as it is: `ScopeKeepsWords(null)` follows `Summary.CanExport` (design §7.1, §13 "No audio track"). Before this, round 1e blocked every untranscribed export with "Keep at least one word before exporting.". `UntranscribedTakeSaysSoAndExplainsTheActions` checks it.
    - A blocked Export clip… from a row also shows its reason as the editor notice, unless a notice is already showing, so mouse users see why nothing opened.
    - `RightBracketFromFullTakeSelectsClipOne` asserts the Full take row's step, so it fails if the row leaves `RowClipStep`.
    - The settings schema's `general.transcriptTextSize` description names the Windows default of 19 (description only), and architecture §12 lists the win-ui test step and the 172-minute limit.

The Mac's `Font.vcDmSans`, `vcGeistMono` and the font name constants stay.

The walk now checks Ctrl+E from a clip row in both of its modes: Export opens with "Selected clips (1)" for `-NormalProject`, and a demo project is refused with the Export button's reason.

- **2026-10-27 — opus-vl-rewrite-budget — Rewrite length budget and Fix with AI:** the lead's brief for this track decided these in-place Core changes on 2026-10-27 (voice-lab §8.1 path 3 and §8.9.1 "Length", "Rewrite request" and "Check"; plan.md ADR-R30 follow-up items Prompt, Request and Contract fixture). The lead's round-two decisions of the same day added the strict JSON reply rule, the Claude Code alias rule, the refusal mapping, the full-request estimate, the prompt-hash binding, malformed edits, deletions and one NFC that never throws (voice-lab §8.1 and §8.9.1, ai.md §2.1 and §12.8). The round-three decisions placed the strict JSON rule in ai.md §4.5 for every reply and transport envelope, and added one exact NFC that keeps U+FFFE, dated snapshot ids, the Claude stop reason and one preflight for both sends (ai.md §2.3, §4.3, §4.5, §5, §6.1, §6.8, §6.9 and §12.8; editing §4.8; voice-lab §3.1, §8.1, §8.9.1 and §10.2). App and CLI have no callers of the changed or new types; only Core tests changed with them. No persisted schema changes.
  - New `VoiceLabTextLength.Length(string text)` in `Domain/VoiceLab/VoiceLabTextLength.cs`, added to the inventory: the number of scalars of general category L* or Nd after NFC (ADR-R19).
  - New internal `EditingAlgorithms.Nfc(string)` is the one exact NFC of editing §4.8, and it never throws. `string.Normalize` rejects an unpaired surrogate and U+FFFE, so it makes only each unpaired surrogate U+FFFD (the new internal `EditingAlgorithms.WellFormed`), then normalises the runs between U+FFFE characters on their own and joins them with U+FFFE, a starter in no composition. It matched Python's NFC on 860,799 probe strings. Every Core NFC goes through it: `CaptionTextRules` `Raw` and `Key`, `EditingSession` corrections, `ProjectValidation`'s NFC check (was `IsNormalized`), `ShareTextRules`, `ShareDisclosure` (main's `ShareDisclosure.WellFormed` is gone), `VoiceLabAlignment`, `VoiceLabTextLength`, `PatchRewriteValidator`, `TightenVerifier` and `EditingImport.CleanWords`. An imported word with U+FFFE now keeps it, as the Mac does.
  - **Consumer action — App owner:** `src/VoxClip.App/ViewModels/EditorViewModel.cs:142` still calls `value.Trim().Normalize()` on a correction, which throws on an unpaired surrogate or U+FFFE before Core sees the text. Core's `EditingSession` already applies `JsTrim` and `EditingAlgorithms.Nfc` to every correction, so the App call can go. App files are outside this track.
  - `VoiceRewriteRequest(Guid RequestId, VoiceRewriteScope Scope, IReadOnlyList<TimedWord> SourceWords, string Instruction, string Language)` → `VoiceRewriteRequest(Guid RequestId, VoiceRewriteScope Scope, IReadOnlyList<TimedWord> SourceWords, string Instruction, string Language, double TargetSeconds, int MinChars, int MaxChars)`. Outside 0 < `TargetSeconds` ≤ 600 (finite) and 1 ≤ `MinChars` ≤ `MaxChars` ≤ 5,000, `PrepareAsync` and `ProposeAsync` throw `VoiceLabException` with `VoiceLabErrorKind.InvalidInput` before a quote or a send. The fingerprint binds all three.
  - `VoiceRewriteProposal(…, string? ProviderRequestId)` → `VoiceRewriteProposal(…, string? ProviderRequestId, int ProposedLength, bool InRange)`. `ProposedLength` is the `VoiceLabTextLength` of `ProposedText`; a proposal outside the range is still returned as it is. `Model` is now the model that answered. A reply with keys besides `rewrite` is accepted (extra keys are ignored), and blankness is `JsTrim`, so a rewrite of only U+FEFF is invalid output.
  - Internal `VoiceRewriteService.RequestText(VoiceRewriteRequest, string, JsonObject)` renders every request string from `request` in `shared/prompts/voice-rewrite.json`, now `promptVersion` 1. A missing template or placeholder throws `VoxClipException` with `SharedResourcesMissing`. The user's request is sent after `JsTrim`, and its block is left out when it is empty.
  - New `PatchRewriteLineRange(string Line, int MinChars, int MaxChars)`, `PatchRewriteRequest(Guid RequestId, IReadOnlyList<ClipWord> Entries, IReadOnlyList<PatchRewriteLineRange> Ranges, string Instruction, string Language)`, `PatchRewriteEdit(string Line, string Text, string Reason, int Length, bool InRange, bool CheckThis, bool IsDeletion)` and `PatchRewriteProposal(Guid Id, IReadOnlyList<PatchRewriteEdit> Edits, int UnknownLines, int DuplicateLines, int MalformedItems, string CheckThisMessage, ConnectorId Connector, string Model, string? ProviderRequestId)` in `Domain/VoiceLab/PatchRewriteContracts.cs`, added to the inventory.
  - New `PatchRewriteService(LlmCatalog catalog)` in `Services/VoiceLab/PatchRewriteService.cs`, added to the inventory: `PrepareAsync(PatchRewriteRequest, ILlmConnector, string model, CancellationToken)` → `Task<VoiceLabPreparedAction>`, `Approve(VoiceLabPreparedAction, bool affirmative)` → `VoiceLabAuthorization`, and `ProposeAsync(PatchRewriteRequest, ILlmConnector, string model, VoiceLabAuthorization, CancellationToken)` → `Task<PatchRewriteProposal>`. `VoiceLabOperation` gains `PatchRewrite`, appended last; nothing switches over the enum. The review endpoint is `patch-rewrite`, and `ContentSha256` covers the transcript block. The send is `LlmFeature.VoiceRewrite` with medium effort, schema `patch_edits` and tag `patch-rewrite`, so it gets no connector retry, beta resend or CLI fallback model.
  - Internal additions: `PatchRewriteValidator` (`Domain/VoiceLab/PatchRewriteValidation.cs`) holds the §8.1 rules: an edit whose `Text` or `Reason` holds a Cc other than tab, or whose `Text` is over 2,000 UTF-8 bytes, is counted in `MalformedItems` with the items structural validation drops; an empty `Text` after `JsTrim` is a deletion with `InRange` true; kept edits over 20,000 bytes of text together are invalid output. `VoiceLabLlmChecks` (`Services/VoiceLab/VoiceLabLlmChecks.cs`) holds `CheckContext` and `StatusFailure`, moved unchanged from `VoiceRewriteService`, and the new `Estimate`, `ReadPrompt`, `AnsweredBy` and `CheckAnswer`.
  - Both rewrite services: `PrepareAsync` no longer rejects the model `default`. The quote binds the prompt file as the SHA-256 of its bytes after CRLF→LF. `VoiceLabEstimate` is `est()` of `System + CachedBlock + Instruction` with unit `token`, basis `unknown` and no `Usd` or `Credits` (before: the source and schema in `estimated input token`). A reply must come from the quoted model by the ai.md §12.8 rule (a Claude Code `sonnet`, `opus` or `haiku` accepts `claude-<alias>-…`, `default` any `claude-…`; an HTTP connector also accepts `<selected>-YYYY-MM-DD` or `<selected>-YYYYMMDD` naming a Gregorian date, through the new `VoiceLabLlmChecks.IsSnapshotDate`; ids compare ordinally), else `InvalidOutput`; then a truncated reply is `Truncated` and a refusal `Refused` with its category (before: `InvalidOutput`).
  - Both rewrite services share one preflight in `VoiceLabLlmChecks` (ai.md §12.8): `IsValidModel` takes 1 to 256 UTF-8 bytes of well-formed text with no Cc (before, a non-blank id of at most 128 UTF-16 units); `MaxOutputTokens(int, int, int?)` is checked in `PrepareAsync` and again before the send, as before; `RequiredTokens` is unchanged, `est()` of the joined text and compact schema plus the cap and 64; and `ContextFits(int, int, double)` needs `context ≥ 512` and `required ≤ ⌊context × factor⌋` (before, a refusal when `required ≥ context × factor` in binary64, so a request needing exactly 90% of the context was refused). `LlmErrors` gains the rewrite copy "The model declined to rewrite this recording." and "The rewrite was incomplete. Please try again."
  - `LlmOutput` (internal) parses strictly for every feature (ai.md §4.5): `TryParse` takes one RFC 8259 value of at most `ReplyMaxDepth` (64) levels, reads every string and number, refuses unpaired surrogate escapes and numbers that are infinite or underflow to zero, and keeps a repeated key's first value; `Extract` uses it, trims with `JsTrim` and treats a one-line fence as empty, and `Validate` re-parses a connector's object and turns a malformed reply into "not JSON" instead of an exception.
  - `LlmOutput.Parse(string)` → `LlmOutput.Envelope(string?)`: the transport envelope of ai.md §4.5, `TryParse(JsTrim(text), EnvelopeMaxDepth)` (512 levels) with no fence or brace scan. HTTP response bodies (`LlmConnector`), the model cache (`LlmModels`) and Codex exec and app-server lines use it (before, `Parse`: 64 levels without `JsTrim`), and so do Claude auth output and the Claude result envelope (before, the reply extraction with its fence and brace scan). `CliLlmAdapter.ParseClaude` now maps `stop_reason` `max_tokens` to `StopKind.Truncated` and `refusal` to `StopKind.Refused(null)`, as the Mac does (before, always `Complete`).
  - Prompts: `shared/prompts/patch-rewrite.json` is new at `promptVersion` 1; rule 5 allows deleting a whole line with empty text (sha256 `696abde9b8d87378d286e754ff57155b659114f8d76db2a6f1f8b07dcb2def0a` after CRLF→LF). `voice-rewrite.json` is at `promptVersion` 1 (`f981955d21d7bbd739c90c5da3597997a315fb8bc503b9f1f83f486d85d4f4c0`).
  - Fixtures, all generated and replayed by both suites: `voice-lab/text-length-cases.json` 36 cases (`f3366ba2658ef7d1a047e492310ed046d03e912892c58387e54b41c053544b48`); `voice-lab/contract.json` 11 `rewriteMutations` rows (`5cc2ebfc8ab91d685e322e7e644fc47f69915c1cdc115c74368b5a8ade6182b9`); `voice-lab/patch-rewrite-cases.json` 72 cases, 35 also replayed through `VoiceRewriteService` (`f81a6c42c45a48813d3444c66509847e012e1748f988ad8f5c6d1d204756cbb6`); `voice-lab/rewrite-checks-cases.json` with 37 answering-model, 16 model-id, 12 output-cap and 12 context rows (`7b9bf5a0a7ce9b7ed5caecf2d1981d824dc70b67298542490bc928361351d998`); `llm/voice-rewrite-requests.json` 7 cases (`d7c527d83edb38e156c34dc93f471a086edad66390a6f09406e2fd04ab666201`) and `llm/patch-rewrite-requests.json` 5 cases (`cce0daae2220a9bef4a738c30d92effa4f3ddf5b266892a2049610fd3f460e3f`), each case with `expected.estimate` and `expected.preflight`; `llm/cli-replies.json` 17 Claude result envelopes (`0f8c451b2bf1d6ae06b2154162ab151ddfc013e325b54f06f79f97f1365914de`); `editing/ingest/clean-words-unicode.json` (`3ab3506bad92a4236bd354f094395316386c5d616ced8358d497e5aa91b32654`).
  - Tests: `Tracks/win-ai/VoiceRewriteBudgetTests.cs` and `PatchRewriteServiceTests.cs` are new, and `CapturingLlmConnector` takes an optional `ConnectorId`, answering model, stop, model list and context. Both services replay the 37 shared answering rows (`OnlyTheQuotedModelMayAnswer`); `SharedRewriteChecks`, `OutputCapIsCheckedWhenTheQuoteIsPrepared`, `QuoteWithoutAPriceAsksForConfirmation` (`ToCostEstimate` reaches `VoiceLabCostPresentation`'s no-price confirmation) and `HttpConnectorTests.ResponseBodyIsOneStrictEnvelope` (7 bodies) are new. `SharedFixtureTests` runs each `cli-reply` case through `ProcessAndCliTests.ClaudeReplyCase`, and the request runners check `expected.preflight` at the smallest context and one token below. `VoiceLabFixtureTests.cs`, `VoiceLabCoreTests.cs` and `SharedVoiceLabRequestFixtureTests.cs` carry the budget, the two request files and their estimates. On the tree merged with main `196b25c7`, the Release build has 0 warnings and the Core tests ran 1,690 with 0 failed and 28 skipped (platform-only, live and informative cases).

- **2026-10-27 — opus-speech-concurrency — Arrival-ordered speech job gate per cache entry and clock-free queue tests:** The Mac failure in hosted CI run 40458357425 came from a polling gate and a test sharing it with parallel suites (see the Mac entry). Windows already queued engine slots with `SemaphoreSlim.WaitAsync`, but the first version of this entry wrongly said its scheduling did not change. Every job first took the per-source `ContentJobs` semaphore and held it while it waited for an engine slot, so a queued local job held back a cloud job for the same source. Keying that semaphore by cache entry fixed this but left two separate waits. A job waiting for its identical job stood outside its kind's queue, so when that job was cancelled or failed, the freed slot went to a job that asked later and the duplicate rejoined the back. The Core API allows this with one service per project; today's App keeps one service at a time, whose project lock already serializes its jobs.
  - `SpeechJobQueues` is now one gate, ported from the Mac `SpeechJobGate`. It keeps a lock-protected list of waiters in arrival order. Each waiter is a `TaskCompletionSource` that runs its continuations asynchronously, and a cancellation registration withdraws a waiter that has not been granted. A job can start when its kind has room and no identical job holds its entry. `Release` frees the entry and the slot together, then grants every waiter that can start, oldest first. A slot granted just before a cancellation stays with its job, which releases it as usual. The members are `EnterAsync(bool onDevice, string entry, CancellationToken)`, `Release(bool onDevice, string entry)`, `Running` and `Waiting`, the jobs holding and waiting for each kind's slots. `ContentJobs` and the two semaphores are gone; `SpeechJobQueues.Shared` and the internal constructor that takes private queues stay as the seam.
  - `TranscribeCoreAsync` works out the raw result entry path (`cachePath`: data root, content key, engine, model, language and raw key prefix) and enters the gate once, with that path and the engine's `OnDevice` kind, before `ReadCacheAsync`, the paid-response recovery and `PreflightCacheAsync`. It releases the gate after post-processing, as the Mac does, so the entry's file, its preflight probe and `PendingPaidResponses[cachePath]` stay guarded per entry. A job served from the cache now also holds a slot of its kind while it reads and post-processes, as on the Mac. `Preparing` 0 is reported when the project's job starts, before the gate.
  - Both apps now follow the per-entry rule of transcription §13.4 (see the Mac entry), also when a job ends without a usable entry: its waiting duplicate keeps its place and starts before any job that asked later.
  - Known gap, older than this track: §13.4 says waiting local jobs show "Waiting for the current transcription…", but neither app reports a waiting state. `TranscriptionStage` (`TranscriptionModels.cs`) has only preparing, uploading, transcribing and aligning, and `SpeechJobQueues.EnterAsync` reports nothing while a job waits. This track does not build it.
  - The queue tests use private queues and no wall-clock bounds. `AppWideQueuesBoundCloudAndLocalConcurrency` runs seven services; at every settled state each kind runs exactly as many jobs as its limit and the remaining jobs allow, only those hold slots, and the rest wait. `AppWideQueuesHandEachReleaseToTheOldestWaiter`, `AppWideQueuesSkipAWaiterWhoseIdenticalJobRuns` (the Mac gate test, ported) and `AppWideQueuesDropACancelledWaiter` test the gate directly. `QueuedLocalJobLetsACloudJobForTheSameSourceRun` and `CloudJobForASourceWithAQueuedLocalJobRunsWithALaterOne` cover the first review. `QueuedDuplicateTakesThePlaceOfItsCancelledTwin` and `DuplicateRunsBeforeALaterJobWhenItsTwinEndsWithoutAnEntry` (cancelled, and a failing engine) replay the second review's two orders on the two cloud slots, where a release can also come while a duplicate's identical job still runs. `HeldJobs` fails a job at once if two identical jobs are inside the engines together.
  - Mutation checks: when `Release` grants each kind's waiters in plain FIFO order and ignores entries, the four ordering tests (`AppWideQueuesSkipAWaiterWhoseIdenticalJobRuns`, `QueuedDuplicateTakesThePlaceOfItsCancelledTwin` and both cases of `DuplicateRunsBeforeALaterJobWhenItsTwinEndsWithoutAnEntry`) fail within seconds, three of them on the identical-jobs check. The same four fail when a duplicate rejoins the back of the queue once its identical job ends, as it did with two waits.
  - Verified on the Mac after merging main at `ca4064f8`, which includes `95e7b98`: `dotnet build VoxClip.CrossPlatform.slnf -c Release --no-incremental` gave 0 warnings and 0 errors. The nine queue cases passed 50 of 50 runs, at 2.6–3.0 s a run. The full Core run, with the shared directory, live tests off and downloads off, ran 1,467 tests: 1,440 passed, 27 skipped (21 that need Windows, 5 live opt-ins and 1 informative caption case), 0 failed.

- **2026-10-27 — opus-win-redesign — Poster frames in VoxClip.Media/Thumbnails (round 1c):** Lead decision, round 1c review: the poster-frame decode stays in VoxClip.Media, because the App never calls FFmpeg, but it leaves win-media's `Pipeline/` folder. `public static class PosterFrames` now lives in `src/VoxClip.Media/Thumbnails/PosterFrames.cs` (namespace `VoxClip.Media.Thumbnails`). Additive API, no existing contract changes: `Candidates(double firstKeptSeconds, double scopeStartSeconds, double scopeSeconds, double sourceSeconds)`, `IsBlack(PixelBuffer frame)` and `ReadJpegAsync(string mediaPath, IReadOnlyList<double> candidates, int rotationDegrees, int coverWidth, int coverHeight, CancellationToken ct)`. It uses the internal `VideoDecoder` (software decode), `MediaThread` and `FfmpegLoader` as they are and never touches `Exporter`, `FrameComposer`, `PreviewEngine` or the `ExportDialog.Export` pipeline. Its only consumer is the App's `Services/Thumbnails.cs`, which caches the JPEG for Home tiles and sidebar rows (design §5.4.7). Tests: `PosterFrameTests` checks the candidate times and the §5.4.7 black-frame rule, and runs everywhere. `NativePosterFrameTests` (`RequiresFFmpeg`) decodes the generated `bars-two-audio.mkv` at a tile's cover size, turns it upright for a 90° rotation, and returns null for the all-black `coarse-clock.mkv`. Hosted Windows CI runs it with the pinned test-only FFmpeg and `VOXCLIP_REQUIRE_NATIVE=1`; the macOS job filters it out by category. The App cache test `PosterFrameComesFromFfmpegAndIsCached` stays in the win-ui project for the VM run.

- **2026-10-27 — opus-media-standins — Per-source audio and envelope cache:** `CacheIndex` (win-editing) adds `EntryDirectory(string)`, `ExtractAudioAsync(IAudioExtractor, MediaInfo, string, IProgress<MediaProgress>?, CancellationToken)`, `RemoveLegacyRootFiles()` (regular root files matching the legacy names and shapes of architecture §8.2 only) and the overload `EvictAsync(long, IReadOnlySet<string>, bool keepEnvelopes, CancellationToken)`. The existing `EvictAsync` keeps its signature and now evicts in two passes (architecture §8.2): entries first shrink to their envelope, `transcripts/` and `meta.json` (one left with neither an envelope nor a raw transcript goes whole), then whole entries go. Stripping an entry without metadata restores its folder's modification time, so it never looks like a fresh extraction to the one-hour orphan rule and Clear everything still removes it. `ExtractAudioAsync` throws the `TouchAsync` `IOException` for an entry that is a link, a reparse point or a file. After the extractor returns, it rewrites `meta.json` under its gate in `CacheJsonContext` format: the existing `CreatedAt`, `LastAccessAt` now, the measured bytes and the envelope. `CacheJsonContext` reads timestamps with any offset (`CacheTimestampConverter`), because `FfmpegAudioExtractor` wrote `+00:00` before `AudioCacheJson` gained `UtcTimestampConverter`. `src/VoxClip.App/Controls/TranscriptionDialog.cs` and `src/VoxClip.Cli/CliOperations.cs` extract through `ExtractAudioAsync`, so `TranscriptionService.ReadEnvelopeAsync` now finds `Cache/<contentKey>/envelope-10ms.f32` and App transcripts come back refined. The App has no Clear cache or Clear everything control and calls no eviction yet. Tests: `CacheEnvelopeTests`.

- **2026-10-27 — opus-media-standins — Caption card core (design §5.4):** `src/VoxClip.Core/Domain/Captions/CaptionCard.cs` adds public `CaptionCardScope`, `CaptionCardSource`, `CaptionCardSurface`, `CaptionCardSurfaces`, `CaptionCardWord` and `CaptionCardText` (win-captions). `CaptionCardText.Create(Project, CaptionCardScope, CaptionStyle, bool wordsVisible, CaptionResources)` is the pure design §5.4.2 rule set, mirrors the macOS `make` and passes the same 51 cases of `shared/fixtures/captions/cards.json`. It measures nothing: callers fit with `MaxLines(surface)` and `DroppingLastWord()` and draw `LayoutWords` with `LayoutHints` at `Time` through `SkiaCaptionRasterizer`. `Time` lies 0.35 × 0.98 into the key word, or in the last word for reveal styles, using `HoldGapSec` (design §5.4.2). Internal `CaptionLayoutBuilder.Cascade` and the selector overload of `CaptionLayoutBuilder.Group` are the §4.4 cascade and the §6.1 pager moved out unchanged. Settings defaults and validation gain `general.showPlaceholderWords` (Boolean, default true). `src/VoxClip.Core/Domain/Captions/MediaStandIn.cs` adds the implementation.md §2 state model: `MediaStatus`, `MediaTranscriptionStage` and `MediaStandIn`. `MediaStandIn.Resolve(MediaStatus, bool hasFrame, CaptionCardText, string styleId, bool holding = false)` is the design §5.4.1 precedence. Views overlay the status and never decide precedence themselves; the 56 `standIns` cases in `cards.json` are shared with macOS. Tests: `CaptionCardTests`. Round-2 verification on the tree merged with main `01e1b387`: the Release build (`--no-incremental`) had 0 warnings; Core passed 1,433 of 1,460 tests with 27 skipped; the dll with `-class` passed `CaptionCardTests`, `CacheEnvelopeTests`, `CacheIndexTests` and `CaptionDomainTests` (149 of 149); `NativeShapingLayoutAndTimelineMatchEveryFixture` passed 86 of 86.

- **2026-10-26 — wave2-vl-request-bodies — Voice Lab request bodies:** the lead approved this in-place Core change on 2026-10-26 for voice-lab §6.5 and §6.7 (own-voice clone), §7.3 (voice changer), §8.5 (alternative TTS request) and §15 tests 12 and 14. The old `ElevenLabsVoiceLab` signatures are replaced, not overloaded; App, CLI and Media have no callers, and only Core tests changed. Old → new:
  - `PrepareClone(string name, byte[] sample, string mimeType, OwnVoicePolicy policy, Guid requestId)` → `PrepareClone(VoiceLabCloneRequest request, OwnVoicePolicy policy, Guid requestId)`.
  - `CreateOwnCloneAsync(string name, byte[] sample, string mimeType, OwnVoicePolicy policy, VoiceLabAuthorization authorization, CancellationToken ct, Guid requestId)` → `CreateOwnCloneAsync(VoiceLabCloneRequest request, OwnVoicePolicy policy, VoiceLabAuthorization authorization, CancellationToken ct, Guid requestId)`.
  - `PrepareSynthesis(string text, string modelId, VoiceLabVoiceChoice voice, string language, Guid requestId)` → `PrepareSynthesis(string text, string modelId, VoiceLabVoiceChoice voice, string language, VoiceLabSpeechOptions options, Guid requestId)`.
  - `SynthesizeAsync(string text, string modelId, VoiceLabVoiceChoice voice, string language, VoiceLabAuthorization authorization, CancellationToken ct, Guid requestId)` → `SynthesizeAsync(string text, string modelId, VoiceLabVoiceChoice voice, string language, VoiceLabSpeechOptions options, VoiceLabAuthorization authorization, CancellationToken ct, Guid requestId)`.
  - `PrepareConversion(byte[] sourceWav, string modelId, VoiceLabVoiceChoice voice, string language, Guid requestId)` → `PrepareConversion(byte[] sourceWav, string modelId, VoiceLabVoiceChoice voice, string language, uint seed, bool cleanAudioOn, Guid requestId)`.
  - `ConvertAsync(byte[] sourceWav, string modelId, VoiceLabVoiceChoice voice, string language, VoiceLabAuthorization authorization, CancellationToken ct, Guid requestId)` → `ConvertAsync(byte[] sourceWav, string modelId, VoiceLabVoiceChoice voice, string language, uint seed, bool cleanAudioOn, VoiceLabAuthorization authorization, CancellationToken ct, Guid requestId)`.

NextText = null)`, `VoiceLabCloneSampleRole` (`ConsentStatement`, `Training`), `VoiceLabCloneSample(VoiceLabCloneSampleRole Role, byte[] Wav)` and `VoiceLabCloneRequest(string DisplayName, string Language, IReadOnlyList<VoiceLabCloneSample> Samples, DateTimeOffset CreatedAt, bool RemoveBackgroundNoise)`.

- **2026-10-26 — lead — paired Voice Lab Core integration:** reviewed Mac Core `aabf7f1` and Windows Core `458e5be` now share the exact rewrite prompt and version-1 behavior fixture, including all 31 conversion-input cases. This closes the earlier Core handoff; consent capture, calibrated speaker verification, generated-media planning/persistence, UI and lip sync remain required. No project schema or media path changes are included.

- **2026-10-26 — win-ai — Voice Lab rewrite dispatch preflight:** `VoiceRewriteService.PrepareAsync` remains local. `ProposeAsync` verifies the service-issued exact-input approval before discovery, checks current connector readiness, selected model, actual context (with selected/curated metadata then a 4,096-token fallback) and complete-source output capacity, and atomically claims approval immediately before its one send. Failed nonbillable preflight leaves approval usable; mutation, cancellation before discovery, ambiguous send and replay still prevent another paid dispatch. The accepted shared prompt and fixture files remain unchanged. The missing-Codex regression now isolates catalog search paths and fake process lookup, so a host-installed CLI cannot change the missing-install result. No production CLI detection change.

- **2026-10-25 — win-ai — Voice Lab Core R1 implementation:** additive `Domain/VoiceLab` contracts and `Services/VoiceLab` transport/rewrite services implement Voice Lab §2–3, §6–8 and the clarified `doc/plan.md` passage/full-take scope. `ElevenLabsVoiceLab` uses the existing `IKeyProvider`, `ILog`, closed region table and nonredirecting Core transport; its handler injection is internal test-only. Models, subscription and bounded paginated voices are discovered only on caller initiation. A service-local prepared action binds the region, endpoint, selected model/voice/language, content hash, consent-policy assertion and fresh unknown-cost estimate, then is claimed before any paid send. Own-clone policy values are inputs from future consent capture/speaker verification, not implementations of either. Stock choices allow only premade and owner-generated categories. Timestamped TTS returns validated signed PCM16LE mono 24 kHz and original-text character alignment; conversion accepts structurally valid PCM16 mono WAV and returns no fabricated alignment. Valid raw PCM may begin `FF FF`. `VoiceRewriteService` uses `shared/prompts/voice-rewrite.json` when present, binds complete source words/timings and one connector/model, and returns an inert review proposal. `LlmFeature.VoiceRewrite` alone disables connector retries, beta compatibility resends and CLI fallback; existing LLM paths keep their retry policy. No project schema, shared fixture, UI, media or public constructor changes. **Integration pending:** merge the reviewed Mac R2 shared prompt/behavior fixture commit when the lead supplies its exact hash, then run the cross-platform vectors. This entry records Core capability only, not a complete workflow.

- **2026-10-25 — win-captions — CLI choice parity:** `CaptionLayoutRequest.WordHints` is an optional, source-word-indexed list of `CaptionWordHints(Emphasis, Emoji)` for captions §9.1–9.2. The public `CaptionEngine.BuildAsync` now carries those manual choices into the existing layout builder, with a count check. The `render-caption` CLI reads the fixture fields through that public path. Gallery tiles use English for keyword selection, as the shared tile fixture generator does; ordinary export calls retain their transcript language. The fixture conformance suite uses the same public path, and focused CLI cases cover manual emoji, manual emphasis, manual exclusion, and the English tile keyword. No persisted project format or renderer path changes.

- **2026-10-25 — win-editing — Planner rebuild cost and CI measurement:** the Windows planner keeps transcript order when word starts and selected cut starts are already nondecreasing, sorting only shuffled inputs with the same original-index tie rules. It builds the end-time speech index only when a cut pause needs it and lets `EditPlan` derive output length and joins once. The 10,000-word/2,000-cut Release test checks three separate 31-rebuild medians against architecture §14's unchanged 10 ms limit and three warmed 10,000-call `JoinGain` passes against the unchanged zero-allocation requirement. Equal-start/equal-end and shuffled-input regression coverage protects sample-exact cut behavior. No public or persisted contract changes (editing §7.1–§7.7; architecture §14).

- **2026-10-25 — win-media — Encoder acceptance for lead review (resolved by ADR-R21):** with the default software `h264_mf` `pc_vbr` settings, the canonical green bar's blue channel is 0 in the source, 1 after the export BGRA/NV12 round trip, and 4 after H.264 decode. The input NV12 centre is Y/U/V 133/64/52; the decoded frame is 134/65/52. Under the former universal decoded RGB ±3 rule, `e2e-8-bars` was a real native failure. `SWS_ACCURATE_RND` left the values unchanged and was reverted; the earlier maximum MF quality experiment also left 4 and was reverted. The dedicated test-only FFmpeg archive runs the AVI, FLV, and raw AAC R5 rows; the source-clean shipping pin does not include those test demuxers.
- **2026-10-25 — win-media — Lead-approved software MF colour refinement:** architecture §§6.6 and 9.5 case 8 and ADR-R21 now require preencoder RGB ±3, independently expected BT.709 limited YUV, decoded Y/U/V each ±1 from the actual submitted NV12, and exact High/yuv420p/BT.709 limited metadata for software `h264_mf`. The production path was measured at 72 bar/position/frame samples: maximum preencoder RGB error 2 and maximum YUV codec delta 1. Hardware Windows and macOS decoded RGB ±3 remain. No public API, persisted format, fixture or codec-setting change.

- **2026-10-25 — win-media / win-captions — W6 integration:** the CLI directly creates `HarfBuzzGlyphShaper` and `SkiaCaptionRasterizer` with the resolved shared root. The default `FrameComposer` creates the same explicit-root rasterizer for preview and export, and the two caption E2E cases now fail on a real rendering defect instead of skipping for an absent branch. Core grants no production Media friend access; no caption model or persisted contract changes.

- **2026-10-25 — win-media — Shared exit-code mapping for lead review:** `VoxClipException.ExitCode` in frozen `Domain/Shared` maps `noAudioTrack`, `decodeFailed` and `ffmpegLicenceGate` to 3, and `keyPermissionMissing` and `mediaTooLong` to 4, matching architecture §9.3 exactly. The CLI is the current consumer; macOS CLI and future UI error handling must keep the same code-to-exit table. This is a shared-contract change already present on this track; no wire codes or project fixtures change.

- **2026-10-25 — win-media / win-ui — Lead-approved, round 4:** `IPreviewEngine.PlaybackFaulted` reports recoverable decode/device faults on the engine owner thread. Its no-op default add/remove accessors keep existing preview implementations source-compatible; `PreviewEngine` raises the real event. The next `PlayAsync` or `SeekAsync` fails once with that fault. `ReadFramesAsync` lifetime semantics are unchanged: the same enumerator continues after recovery, without re-subscription; only disposal or terminal owner failure completes it. The opt-in lease stream has the same lifetime. **win-ui consumer action:** subscribe to the event, marshal the callback to the UI thread with `DispatcherQueue`, and show the recoverable fault inline using the saved-edits copy; unsubscribe when disposing the consumer. Do not replace or complete the frame stream during recovery.

- **2026-10-25 — win-media — Proposed:** central presentation-copy formatting for frozen `Domain/Shared/MediaException` diagnostics remains a skeleton/lead and win-ui integration task (architecture §6.1/§10.2). Preserve NativeError and Operation; present decode failures with the exact saved-edits wrapper. CLI now formats that boundary without changing the frozen type; other media-owned literal messages are corrected in place. No serialized or public signature change is made here.

- **2026-10-25 — win-media / win-ai / win-speech / win-captions — Integration TODO:** only W5, W6 and W7 remain late-bound in `MergeAdapters.cs`; their implementations are still placeholders in this checkout. The adapters target `ClipFinder(LlmRunner, Func<ConnectorId, ILlmConnector>, string sharedRoot)`, `ElevenLabsTranscriber(IKeyProvider, HttpClient)`, `WhisperNetTranscriber(ModelStore)` and `TranscriptionService(IEnumerable<ITranscriber>, DataLocations)`, plus the shared-root model/LLM catalogues and caption shaper/rasterizer. Transcription targets `TranscribeAsync(TranscriptionRequest, AudioEnvelope?, IProgress<TranscriptionProgress>?, CancellationToken)`. CLI providers are environment-only, never DPAPI (architecture §9.4). `ClipFinderGateException.Kind/UserMessage` maps NoWords to `noWords`/exit 3 and NeedInstructions to `needInstructions`/exit 2 without provider retries. Missing APIs fail closed and `TODO(merge)` marks their direct-call replacement. Consumers: root export, find-clips, transcribe, render-caption; no persisted/public contract changes. Lead must merge these siblings and rerun black-box CLI parity; boundary tests cover constructor injection, gate mapping and range validation.

- **2026-10-25 — win-core-native — First settings load during replacement:** `SettingsStore.LoadAsync` always attempts the retrying atomic read, even when an initial existence probe misses the destination during `File.Replace`. A successful retry loads the complete saved preferences before the store caches state or accepts a save. After the existing 25/50/100/200/400 ms retry schedule, genuinely absent first-launch settings return defaults; an initially visible file that stays unreadable reports an explicit read error. Permission errors and cancellation never become defaults, and strict UTF-8 damage still enters recovery. The missing-file first launch can add 775 ms when the settings directory already exists; no separate wait or retry schedule was added (architecture §2.5; editing §10.2).

- **2026-10-25 — win-core-native — Native replacement follow-up:** repeated native execution also observed `FileNotFoundException` opening the destination during `File.Replace`, after the reader-side sharing violation was addressed. The shared settings/project reader therefore retries Win32 name-not-found (2), sharing violation (32) and lock violation (33), still with five delays totaling 775 ms. Missing documents still fail after exhaustion; malformed data and other I/O errors are never retried. Injected error-2 success/exhaustion cases run on every host. Other native failures in untouched region/TLS tests and the allocation assertion are reported to their owners rather than changed here.

Missing prerequisites produce an explicit skip unless `VOXCLIP_REQUIRE_SCHEMA_PYTHON=1`, which fails. The lead must set the require flag in both `.NET core tests (macOS)` and Windows `.NET core tests` CI jobs (architecture §12); no CI files changed here.

Writer replacement/recovery remains unchanged; reader and writer backoffs are 25/50/100/200/400 ms (775 ms total), cancellation-aware. The concurrent settings test uses the identical product reader policy; injected tests verify exhaustion/nonretryable failures and held readers verify whole old/new documents for settings and projects (editing §10.2).

Both preserve pause breath and derive the new threshold from `Refined` (editing §10.5); consumers must obtain the specified destructive-operation confirmation first. No existing signature or wire shape changes. `EditPlan` caches its binary-searched gain parts once, rebuilding on record copies that change the sample rate, total samples or kept intervals. `Joins` now includes both clip edges even for a single kept part or a zero-length fade. `EditingFormat.ToFixed1` remains the only public toFixed1 symbol and uses JS exponent form at ≥1e21. Saves retain the newest ten AI runs overall plus referenced older runs. Corrupt settings are preserved beside the original as `settings.json.corrupt-<unique-id>` and defaults are returned so later saves work; update links are HTTPS GitHub URLs only. Regression coverage includes join-table/gain parity at fixture probes and every sample of single-range clips, copy-safe cached gains, reducer limits, transcript replacement and a warmed 10,000-word/2,000-cut plan rebuild median below architecture §14's 10 ms budget (31 measurements; p95 logged separately because runtime/OS scheduling outliers are not deterministic). The existing project save gate serializes snapshots from one document. The optional project title accepts an empty string, matching the schema's lack of a minimum length. These are lead-requested spec/schema conformance fixes, not fixture changes; no frozen common tests were edited.

### 2026-10-26 — Reviewed Windows update restart save boundary

### 2026-10-27 — wave2-vl-audio-math — Voice Lab audio math in Core

- `src/VoxClip.Core/Domain/VoiceLab/VoiceLabLoudness.cs` adds the pure measurements every v3 recipe needs, on mono Float32 samples that callers downmix: `VoicedRmsDb(ReadOnlySpan<float>, int) → double?`; `NeighbourhoodVoicedRmsDb(ReadOnlySpan<float>, int, int windowStart, int windowEnd) → double` for the source level beside a window; `LevelGainDb(double?, double?, double)` and `LevelGainDb(ReadOnlySpan<float>, int, int windowStart, int windowEnd, ReadOnlySpan<float>, int) → double` for `audio.gainDb`; `SamplePeakDbfs(ReadOnlySpan<float>)` for `qa.peakDbfs`; `TruePeakDbtp(ReadOnlySpan<float>, int)`; `MomentaryLufs(ReadOnlySpan<float>, int, int windowStart)`; `SeamJumpLu(ReadOnlySpan<float>, int, int)` and `LufsJumpLu(ReadOnlySpan<float>, int, IReadOnlyList<int>)` for `qa.lufsJumpLu`; and `Flatness(ReadOnlySpan<float>, int)` for §8.6 ranking. Failures throw `VoiceLabAudioMathException` with `VoiceLabAudioMathErrorCode` `NoVoicedAudio`, `InsufficientContext` or `InvalidInput`.
- `VoiceLabConform.cs` adds `VoiceLabConform.Decide(AudioEnvelope, AudioEnvelope, long inputSamples, long outputSamples, int sampleRate, double searchSeconds) → VoiceLabConformDecision`, with `VoiceChangerSearchSeconds` (0.3) and `CleanAudioSearchSeconds` (0.5). The records are `TrimOrPad(long Delta)`, `Shift(long Lag, double Peak)` and `OutOfSync(double Peak, long Lag)`; the last records that the provider cost was already spent. Nothing is time-stretched (ADR-R30).
- The rules match the Mac Core entry of the same date and `shared/fixtures/voice-lab/audio-math-README.md`: rounded 10 ms frames voiced above −45 dBFS RMS, the source level from the 3 s on each side of the window, excluding it, the static ±12 dB gain capped at −1 dBTP, the BS.1770-4 Annex 2 true peak, never below the sample peak, libebur128's per-rate K-weighting derivation within 1e-6 of the published 48 kHz values, 400 ms momentary loudness, seam windows each floored at −70 LUFS, 100 ms flatness steps and the §4.4 conform decision with its minimum overlap of half the shorter envelope.
- Round 2 settled the round-1 spec questions and fixed the round-1 review findings, as the Mac entry describes. No `TODO(wave2-vl-audio-math)` remains.
- `tests/VoxClip.Core.Tests/Tracks/voice-lab/VoiceLabAudioMathTests.cs` reads all 92 cases of `shared/fixtures/voice-lab/audio-math-cases.json` (SHA-256 `8ff014b4949f7476a0640ad94f455115f634d3efd1f6eb1e736d741bd34d7a34`); every metric matched the NumPy reference exactly. The 60 s, 48 kHz pass took 0.14–0.19 s in Release; it runs alone in the `voice-lab timing` collection and must finish within 2 s.

### 2026-10-26 — opus-win-audio-device — Preview plays muted without a usable audio output device

- Hosted Windows CI run 40397271859 failed `NativePreviewPlaysPausesSeeksAndReleasesSynthLease` with `CoreAudioException` 0x80070490 (E_NOTFOUND): the runner has no default render endpoint, so `PreviewEngine.PlayAsync` threw. A PC with no output device (an unplugged headset, Remote Desktop without audio redirection, a server SKU) failed the same way. Per the lead's decision, preview now keeps playing video without sound.
- **Public additions requested by the lead's brief:** `IPreviewEngine.AudioStatusChanged` (`event EventHandler<PreviewAudioStatus>?` with no-op default accessors, like `PlaybackFaulted`) and `enum PreviewAudioStatus { Available, NoOutputDevice, OutputInUse }` in `Services/Media/MediaContracts.cs`. The engine raises the event on its owner thread only when the status changes. No existing signature, JSON name or enum value changed, and `DemoPreviewEngine` compiles unchanged. Internally, `PreviewEngine` takes a `createAudio` factory for the internal `IPreviewAudioOutput` seam, which `WasapiAudioPlayer` implements explicitly, and `WasapiAudioPlayer` takes an internal `IWasapiOutput` factory over NAudio's `WasapiPlayer` (`SharedWasapiOutput` in production). Its public members are unchanged.
- **Behaviour:** an output error is *unusable* when the default render endpoint is missing or gone (E_NOTFOUND, AUDCLNT_E_DEVICE_INVALIDATED, AUDCLNT_E_RESOURCES_INVALIDATED, AUDCLNT_E_ENDPOINT_CREATE_FAILED, AUDCLNT_E_SERVICE_NOT_RUNNING) or another app holds it in exclusive mode (AUDCLNT_E_DEVICE_IN_USE). **At a start** (Play, or resuming after a seek or plan change), an unusable error releases the audio reader and device, keeps video on the `Stopwatch` clock and reports `NoOutputDevice`, or `OutputInUse` for AUDCLNT_E_DEVICE_IN_USE. Any other device error fails that start: `PlayAsync`, `SeekAsync` or `UpdateSnapshotAsync` throws it, playback stays stopped and no event is raised. **During playback**, an unusable error gets exactly one immediate reopen of the current default endpoint from the last played frame, so unplugging headphones moves sound to the speakers with no status change. If the reopen finds no usable device, playback continues muted from that frame with the matching status. Neither hand-over steps the clock back or jumps it forward. Any other device error during playback, including one from the reopen, stops playback at that frame and raises `PlaybackFaulted` once, and the next Play or Seek rethrows it once. A pause, seek or plan change that sees a loss or fault before the owner's clock tick handles it exactly the same way, including a loss that only the device's pause call reports, and the call itself completes: a pause holds at the lost frame, and a seek or plan swap is applied and resumes only when nothing faulted, so the Windows editor keeps its preview. The status event fires only on change, and every later start retries the device; there is no polling or device-change subscription. NAudio 3.3 ends its render thread when the device is lost and then reports position 0 instead of throwing. `WasapiAudioPlayer` records the `PlaybackStopped` error and keeps its frame clock monotonic, so the loss is detected instead of the clock snapping back to the play origin and freezing.
- **App:** only `src/VoxClip.App/Pages/EditorPage.xaml.cs` changed. It subscribes and unsubscribes next to `PlaybackFaulted`, shows "No audio output device. Playing without sound." or "Another app is using the audio output. Playing without sound." in the existing `EditorNotice` InfoBar, and clears only those notices when audio returns. The proposed design.md §13 rows for both statuses and the architecture.md §6.3 text are with the lead; `doc/` is unchanged.
- **Export is unaffected:** `WasapiAudioPlayer` is the only NAudio consumer and only `PreviewEngine` uses it. `Exporter`, `EditedWaveWriter`, the CLI and `ExportDialog` never open an output device. The device-less hosted run exported through the CLI (336,000 audio samples) and passed every other native export test.
- **Tests:** `NativePreviewPlaysPausesSeeksAndReleasesSynthLease`, the only Media test that opens a real output device, now calls `NativeTest.RequireAudioOutput()`, which has its own flag, `VOXCLIP_REQUIRE_AUDIO_OUTPUT`. `verify-windows.ps1` sets it to `1` for its full VM run (the `tests` step, which runs the whole Media suite) and its shipping-acceptance run (the new `shipping-preview-audio` step, which runs this test against the shipping DLLs). `verify-vm.sh` passes it to both, so either run fails without a default render endpoint. Hosted CI never sets it and skips the test with a remedy that names the flag. It also fails if any muted status appears. Portable tests: `PreviewWithoutOutputDevicePlaysMutedAndReleasesSynthLease`; `PreviewWithOutputInUsePlaysMutedThroughSeekAndPlanChange` (AUDCLNT_E_DEVICE_IN_USE at the first Play and at a seek and a plan change while playing, then a change to `NoOutputDevice`); `PreviewFatalDeviceErrorFailsTheStartWithoutMuting` (a fatal error opening the endpoint or at Init); `WasapiAudioPlayerTests`, which drives the `PlaybackStopped` capture, the `lost` break and the monotonic frame guard through a fake `IWasapiOutput`; and `NativeEvidenceGateTests` for the three flags. FFmpeg-gated, device-independent tests, which hosted CI runs: `PreviewLostOutputReopensTheDefaultDeviceAtTheLostFrame` (an invalidated or in-use loss that reopens, within one 30 fps frame of the lost frame), `PreviewLostOutputWithoutReplacementPlaysMutedUntilTheNextPlay` (three reopen failures; the muted clock stays within one frame of the time since the status) and `PreviewFatalDeviceErrorFailsTheStartAndFaultsPlayback` (Init fails the start, then a mid-play or reopen error raises `PlaybackFaulted` at the last played frame without muting). `PreviewLossFirstSeenByACommandIsHandledAsOnTheOwnerTick` (16 cases) holds the owner inside a frame so a pause, seek or plan change sees the loss first, or fails only the device's pause call, and crosses each with a reopen that opens, fails as unavailable, fails as in-use or fails fatally. Every case asserts that the call completes, at most one `PlaybackFaulted`, the rethrow once, exactly one reopen and the playhead within one frame. `PreviewPauseFailureDisposesProvisionalSynthSessionAndKeepsOldGraph` became `PreviewPauseFailureDuringPlanChangeFaultsOnceAndStillSwapsTheGraph`, because a pause fault no longer fails the plan change. `PreviewLivenessTests` is unchanged from the base.
- Round 1, verified on the Mac: the Release cross-platform build had zero warnings and errors. Core: 1,402 tests, 1,375 passed, 27 skipped (a rerun; the first run hit the timing-sensitive `HttpConnectorTests.TimeoutIsDistinctFromCancellationAndNeitherRetries`, which failed once in three isolated runs on the cold first run and is unrelated). Portable Media: 462 tests, 458 passed, 4 opt-in skips. ### 2026-10-26 — Voice Lab v3 upgrade, own-voice references and asset naming (wave2-vl-project-v3)

- `VoiceLabProject.UpgradeToV3(project)` is the only promotion to schema v3 (voice-lab.md §10.1, §10.5). Version 1 and segment-free version 2 projects gain empty `Voices` and `VideoAssets` and Clean audio off, and version 2 keeps its assets. Version 3 returns the same instance, and any version 2 segment throws the new `LegacySegmentsNeedReview`. Loading and saving never upgrade. `Accept(project, newAssets, segment)` upgrades version 1 and segment-free version 2 first, so it never writes version 2. A recipe-less segment now fails v3 validation with `MissingRenderPolicy`. `VoiceLabRenderValidation` now reports a missing segment scope as `MissingRenderPolicy`, as Mac does, and keeps `InvalidSegment` for a scope value outside the enum. The shared case `accept-recipe-less-take-into-v1` pins the code on both platforms.
- `VoiceLabRenderValidation` now checks the project.schema.json shape of every stored recipe, rejected or not, of any kind or voice source. The checks include:
  - ID patterns;
  - non-empty chunks and region;
  - the provenance provider pattern, and a non-empty endpoint, model and voice ID;
  - the cost quote ID, and a `Usd` of at least 0;
  - finite numbers, and `TranscriptTokens` of at least 0;
  - `TranscriptMatch`, `SpeakerScore` and `SpeakerThreshold` within [0, 1];
  - a non-empty `SpeakerModel` when one is present;
  - defined enum values.

  For each candidate in order, an unrejected candidate first passes render policy, then every stored recipe passes the shape check; the first failure is reported, and a shape failure is `InvalidSegment`. As a result, `Accept` cannot return a project that fails the schema, or one on which `ProjectSerialization.Serialize` throws `ArgumentException` for a non-finite number. Ten shared accept cases pin the shape rules. The two-defect cases `accept-two-defects-earlier-candidate-first` (`InvalidSegment`) and `accept-two-defects-policy-before-shape` (`QaRejected`) pin the order.
- Render policy also refuses an unrejected candidate whose recipe `Endpoint` contains `://` (ordinal), with `InvalidSegment`, the code of the segment endpoint rule. `SelectCandidate` copies that provenance into the segment, so `Accept` no longer stores an alternative that can never be selected. The shared case `accept-alternative-endpoint-url` pins it.
- Duplicate voice-reference IDs fail with `ConsentUnverified`, and Mac now reports the same code. The shared case `load-duplicate-voice-reference-ids` pins it on both platforms.
- `AddVoiceReference(project, reference)` and `MarkVoiceDeleted(project, id)` add an ElevenLabs consent reference and mark it deleted. On version 1 or 2 they throw the new `RequiresVersion3`. Invalid references throw `InvalidVoice`, and missing IDs throw `MissingVoice`. Display names are counted in runes, not UTF-16 units. Deletion keeps the reference, segments and assets: earlier own-voice takes still validate and prepare, while a new own-voice accept throws `ConsentUnverified`. `Accept` checks every unrejected own-voice candidate, not only the selected one. `SelectCandidate` does not check voice status, so an alternative accepted while consent was active can still be selected after deletion. Marking a deleted reference again returns the same instance. These transitions return values without I/O or undo registration (§10.3).
- **Consumer action — wave2-vl-media-win:** name new paid assets with `VoiceLabAssetNaming.File(string projectId, string assetId, string fileExtension)`. It returns `<projectId>/<assetId>.wav` or `.flac` or throws `VoiceLabProjectException(InvalidAsset)`. Core validation applies the same rule to stored files and still reports `UnsafePath`. §10.5 video assets also go through it, with a fixed `.mp4` extension, and still report `InvalidAsset`. The public `File` accepts only `wav` and `flac`. The new error codes are appended to `VoiceLabProjectErrorCode`, so existing numeric values are unchanged. The public declaration inventory now lists `VoiceLabAssetNaming`, `VoiceLabProject`, `VoiceLabProjectErrorCode` and `VoiceLabProjectException` in `VoiceLabProject.cs`.
- `shared/fixtures/voice-lab/upgrade-cases.json` (generated by `tools/fixtures/voice-lab/generate_upgrade.py`, described in `upgrade-README.md`) is replayed by `Tracks/voice-lab/VoiceLabUpgradeTests` and the Mac suite. Both suites compare explicit nulls exactly. Mac now writes every `VoiceLabVideoRecipe` key. Development builds before 2026-10-26 omitted the nil ones. `ProjectSerialization` refuses such files as missing required properties, and the raw `ProjectValidation` now also lists all 15 keys as required, explicit null included (architecture §2.1 rule 8). Mac refuses them too. `RecipeVideoMissingAKeyIsUnreadable` pins `ProjectUnreadable` through `ProjectStore`, `ProjectSerialization` and the raw validator. `project.schema.json` now requires all 15 keys. The fixture's `videoRecipes` objects hold the Mac encoding of each video mode, and `MacEncodedVideoRecipesDecodeInEveryMode` reads each one back unchanged.
- Verification ran on the Mac host on the merge with main `307c93b`, with `VOXCLIP_SHARED_DIR`, `VOXCLIP_SCHEMA_PYTHON` and `VOXCLIP_REQUIRE_SCHEMA_PYTHON=1`, live tests off and a temporary `VOXCLIP_DATA_DIR`. `dotnet build VoxClip.CrossPlatform.slnf -c Release` had 0 warnings and 0 errors. Core tests passed 1,390 with 27 explicit skips (1,417 total). A first run under a host load average of 600 to 900 failed four timing tests outside this track: `ReviewProcessTests.SilentAppServerHonorsTimeoutAndCancel` and three `RegionSafetyTests` regions. In the same run, one store load in `RecipeVideoMissingAKeyIsUnreadable` hit the raw validator's 1 s regex timeout. That test now loads through the store only twice, and the rerun passed. On their own, this track's `VoiceLabUpgradeTests` and `VoiceLabProjectTests` passed 22 of 22.
